	try {
		let pathSegments = [];
		try {
			pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split("/").filter((segment) => segment !== "" && segment !== "global").slice(0, 2);
		} catch {
			pathSegments = [];
		}
		const matchedLang = pathSegments.map((seg) => validLangs.find((lang) => lang.toLowerCase() === seg.toLowerCase())).find((lang) => lang !== void 0);
		const candidates = [];
		if (matchedLang) candidates.push(matchedLang);
		else candidates.push(defaultLang);
		candidates.push("global");
		let content = void 0;
		let usedLang = void 0;
		for (const key of candidates) {
			if (!key) continue;
			const banner = bannerByLocale[key];
			if (!banner?.content) continue;
			content = banner.content;
			usedLang = key;
			break;
		}
		if (!content) {
			document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
			return;
		}
		let shouldShow = true;
		let i = 0;
		while (i < localStorage.length) {
			const key = localStorage.key(i);
			i++;
			if (!key?.endsWith(bannerDismissedKey)) continue;
			const langSpecificDismissal = localStorage.getItem(key);
			if (!!langSpecificDismissal && langSpecificDismissal === content) {
				shouldShow = false;
				break;
				//!key.startsWith("lang:")) makes this backwards compatible with removing legacy local storage
			} else if (usedLang && (key.startsWith(`lang:${usedLang}_`) || !key.startsWith("lang:"))) {
				localStorage.removeItem(key);
				i--;
			}
		}
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, shouldShow ? "visible" : "hidden");
	} catch (e) {
		console.error(e);
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
	}
})(

	function setIsOpaque() {
		const offset = 50;
		let isOpaque = false;
		if (window.scrollY > offset) isOpaque = true;
		else isOpaque = false;
		const el = document.getElementById(navbarTransitionId);
		if (!el || !el.hasAttribute("data-is-opaque")) return;
		el.setAttribute("data-is-opaque", `${!!isOpaque}`);
	}
	/** https://github.com/pacocoursey/next-themes/blob/a7eeabc39cfb37d74ea3d82eac674d0e1851b1cb/next-themes/src/index.tsx#L234-L253 */
	function disableAnimation() {
		const css = document.createElement("style");
		const scope = `#${navbarTransitionId}`;
		css.appendChild(document.createTextNode(`${scope},${scope} *,${scope} *::before,${scope} *::after{-webkit-transition:none!important;-moz-transition:none!important;-o-transition:none!important;-ms-transition:none!important;transition:none!important}`));
		document.head.appendChild(css);
		return function() {
			window.getComputedStyle(document.body);
			setTimeout(() => {
				document.head.removeChild(css);
			}, 1);
		};
	}
	const restoreAnimation = disableAnimation();
	function done() {
		window.removeEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque);
		document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
		setIsOpaque();
		restoreAnimation();
	}
	window.addEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque, { passive: true });
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
	("requestAnimationFrame" in globalThis ? requestAnimationFrame : setTimeout)(setIsOpaque);
	if (document.readyState === "complete") done();
	else window.addEventListener("load", done, { once: true });
})("navbar-transition")</script><script id="_mintlify-scroll-margin-script">(function () {
  var measure = function measureScrollMarginTop() {
	const chromeElements = document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]");
	let chromeBottom = 0;
	chromeElements.forEach((element) => {
		const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
		if (rect.height > 0) chromeBottom = Math.max(chromeBottom, rect.bottom);
	});
	let containerTop = 0;
	const contentContainer = document.getElementById("content-container");
	if (contentContainer) {
		const { overflowY } = getComputedStyle(contentContainer);
		if (overflowY === "auto" || overflowY === "scroll") containerTop = Math.max(0, contentContainer.getBoundingClientRect().top);
	}
	const scrollMt = `${Math.round(Math.max(0, chromeBottom - containerTop)) + 40}px`;
	const rootStyle = document.documentElement.style;
	if (rootStyle.getPropertyValue("--scroll-mt") !== scrollMt) rootStyle.setProperty("--scroll-mt", scrollMt);
};
  (function watchScrollMarginTop(measure) {
	let observed = [];
	let frame = 0;
	const resizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined" ? null : new ResizeObserver(schedule);
	function run() {
		const elements = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]"));
		if ((elements.length !== observed.length || elements.some((element, index) => element !== observed[index])) && resizeObserver) {
			resizeObserver.disconnect();
			elements.forEach((element) => resizeObserver.observe(element));
		}
		observed = elements;
		measure();
	}
	function schedule() {
		if (frame) return;
		frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			frame = 0;
			run();
		});
	}
	const chromeSelector = "header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]";
	function touchesChrome(mutation) {
		const target = mutation.target;
		if (target instanceof Element && target.closest(chromeSelector)) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.addedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.removedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		return false;
	}
	new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
		if (mutations.some(touchesChrome)) schedule();
	}).observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	window.addEventListener("resize", schedule);
	if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", run);
	run();
})(measure);
  (function pinInitialHash(measure) {
	const hash = window.location.hash.slice(1);
	if (!hash) return;
	function isSmooth() {
		return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).scrollBehavior === "smooth";
	}
	function pin(behavior) {
		const target = document.getElementById(hash);
		if (!target) return false;
		measure();
		target.scrollIntoView(behavior ? { behavior } : void 0);
		return true;
	}
	if (document.readyState === "complete") {
		pin();
		return;
	}
	const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
		if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
	});
	function stop() {
		observer.disconnect();
		window.removeEventListener("load", onLoad);
	}
	function settle() {
		const inputEvents = [
			"wheel",
			"touchstart",
			"keydown",
			"pointerdown"
		];
		let ticks = 0;
		let lastY = window.scrollY;
		const interval = setInterval(() => {
			ticks += 1;
			const target = document.getElementById(hash);
			if (!target || ticks >= 6) return done();
			const scrolling = Math.abs(window.scrollY - lastY) > 1;
			lastY = window.scrollY;
			if (scrolling) return;
			const margin = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(target).scrollMarginTop) || 0;
			if (Math.abs(target.getBoundingClientRect().top - margin) > 4) pin();
		}, 350);
		function done() {
			clearInterval(interval);
			inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.removeEventListener(event, done));
		}
		inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.addEventListener(event, done, { passive: true }));
	}
	function onLoad() {
		const smooth = isSmooth();
		stop();
		if (smooth) requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			pin();
			settle();
		});
	}
	window.addEventListener("load", onLoad);
	observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
})(measure);
})();</script><noscript><iframe src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/ns.html?id=GTM-52W7VM2" height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript><a href="#content-area" class="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:top-2 focus:left-2 focus:z-50 focus:p-2 focus:text-sm focus:bg-background-light dark:focus:bg-background-dark focus:rounded-md focus:outline-primary dark:focus:outline-primary-light">Skip to main content</a><script>(function assistantScriptFn(storageKey, widthKey, cssVar, hasChatPermissions, isAssistantUiV2) {

    }
    var isOpen = JSON.parse(raw);
    var widthRaw = localStorage.getItem(widthKey);
    var width = widthRaw !== null ? JSON.parse(widthRaw) : defaultWidth;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, isOpen ? width + 'px' : '0px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', isOpen ? 'open' : 'closed');
  } catch (e) {
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(cssVar, '0px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-assistant-state', 'closed');
  }
})(
    "chat-assistant-sheet-open",
    "chat-assistant-sheet-width",
    "--assistant-sheet-width",
    true,
    false
  )</script><div class="max-lg:contents lg:flex lg:w-full" data-docs-theme="mint" data-assistant-layout="legacy"><div data-assistant-sheet-container="" class="sticky shrink-0 z-22 lg:order-last bg-background-light dark:bg-background-dark mt-(--banner-height,0px) top-(--banner-height,0px) h-[calc(100vh-var(--banner-height,0px))] max-lg:hidden print:hidden"   try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content') && matches[j].getClientRects().length) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var row = item.querySelector(':scope > button') || item;
    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center relative gap-2 min-w-0"><h1 id="page-title" class="text-3xl sm:text-4xl text-gray-900 tracking-tight dark:text-gray-200 <h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="parameters"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#parameters" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Parameters</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="name"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#name" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>name</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>name</code> parameter serves as the identifier of a cube. It must be unique among
<em>all cubes and views</em> within a deployment and follow the <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/syntax#naming" class="link">naming
conventions</a>.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="sql_alias"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#sql_alias" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>sql_alias</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use <code>sql_alias</code> when auto-generated cube alias prefix is too long and truncated
by databases such as Postgres:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" class="min-w-0 flex-1 w-0 rounded-tl-xl"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_18h5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content"><div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" role="tablist" aria-label="Code examples" class="text-xs leading-6 gap-1 flex"><button type="button" data-active="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" aria-disabled="false" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="true" id="base-ui-_R_5l8h5deehkd74t9_" data-composite-item-active="" class="group flex items-center <span data-as="p">It’ll generate aliases for members such as <code>order_facts__count</code>. <code>sql_alias</code> affects
all member names including pre-aggregation table names.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="extends"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#extends" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>extends</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">You can use the <code>extends</code> parameter to <a href="/docs/data-modeling/extending-cubes" class="link">extend cubes</a> in order to reuse
all declared members of a cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">In the example below, <code>extended_order_facts</code> will reuse the <code>sql</code> and <code>count</code> measures
from <code>order_facts</code>:</span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DCDCAA;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">cube</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">`extended_order_facts`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  extends:</span><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> order_facts</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  measures:</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    double_count:</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      type:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `number`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      sql:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">${</span><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">count</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> * 2`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">You can also omit the cube name while defining a cube in JavaScript. This way,
Cube doesn’t register this cube globally; instead it returns a reference which
you can use while combining cubes. It makes sense to use it for dynamic data
model generation and reusing with <code>extends</code>. Previous example without defining
<code>order_facts</code> cube globally:</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="data_source"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#data_source" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>data_source</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Each cube can have its own <code>data_source</code> name to support scenarios where data
should be fetched from multiple databases. The value of the <code>data_source</code>
parameter will be passed to the <a href="/reference/configuration/config#driver_factory" class="link"><code>driverFactory()</code></a>
function as part of the <code>context</code> parameter. By default, each cube has a
<code>default</code> value for its <code>data_source</code>; to override it you can use:</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="sql"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#sql" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>sql</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>sql</code> parameter specifies the SQL that will be used to generate a table that
will be queried by a cube. It can be any valid SQL query, but usually it takes
the form of a <code>SELECT * FROM my_table</code> query. Please note that you don’t need to
use <code>GROUP BY</code> in a SQL query on the cube level. This query should return a
plain table, without aggregations.</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" class="min-w-0 flex-1 w-0 rounded-tl-xl"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_19b5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" <span data-as="p">It is recommended to prefer the <a href="#sql_table" class="link"><code>sql_table</code></a> parameter
over the <code>sql</code> parameter for all cubes that are supposed to use queries like
this: <code>SELECT * FROM table</code>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="sql_table"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#sql_table" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>sql_table</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>sql_table</code> parameter is used as a concise way for defining a cube that uses
a query like this: <code>SELECT * FROM table</code>. Instead of using the
<a href="#sql" class="link"><code>sql</code></a> parameter, use <code>sql_table</code> with the table name that this
cube will query.</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" class="min-w-0 flex-1 w-0 rounded-tl-xl"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_19n5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content"><div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" role="tablist" aria-label="Code examples" class="text-xs leading-6 gap-1 flex"><button type="button" data-active="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" aria-disabled="false" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="true" id="base-ui-_R_5l9n5deehkd74t9_" data-composite-item-active="" class="group flex items-center relative gap-1.5 my-1 mb-1.5 outline-0 whitespace-nowrap font-medium ml-0! first:ml-2.5! focus-visible:outline-hidden text-primary-light"><div class="peer/title flex items-center gap-1.5 px-1.5 rounded-lg z-10 group-focus-visible:outline-1 group-focus-visible:outline-primary-light group-hover:bg-gray-700/70 group-hover:text-primary-light">YAML</div><div class="absolute -bottom-1.5 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary dark:bg-primary-light peer-empty/title:hidden"></div></button><button type="button" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" aria-disabled="false" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="false" id="base-ui-_R_9l9n5deehkd74t9_" class="group flex items-center relative gap-1.5 my-1 mb-1.5 outline-0 whitespace-nowrap <h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="title"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#title" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>title</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use the <code>title</code> parameter to change the display name of the cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">By default, Cube will humanize the cube’s name, so for instance, <code>users_orders</code>
would become <code>Users Orders</code>. If default humanizing doesn’t work in your case,
please use the <code>title</code> parameter. It is highly recommended to give human readable
names to your cubes. It will help everyone on a team better understand the data
structure and will help maintain a consistent set of definitions across an
organization.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="description"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#description" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>description</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This parameter provides a human-readable description of a cube.
When applicable, it will be displayed in <a href="/docs/explore-analyze/playground" class="link">Playground</a> and exposed
to data consumers via <a href="/reference" class="link">APIs and integrations</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">A description can give a hint both to your team and end users, making sure they
interpret the data correctly.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="public"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#public" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>public</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>public</code> parameter is used to manage the visibility of a cube. Valid values
for <code>public</code> are <code>true</code> and <code>false</code>. When set to <code>false</code>, this cube <strong>cannot</strong>
be queried through the API. Defaults to <code>true</code>.</span>

<span data-as="p">To learn more about using <code>public</code> to control visibility based on security
context, read the <a href="/docs/data-modeling/access-control" class="link">Controlling access to cubes and views
recipe</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="refresh_key"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#refresh_key" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>refresh_key</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Cube’s caching layer uses <code>refresh_key</code> queries to get the current version of
content for a specific cube. If a query result changes, Cube will invalidate all
queries that rely on that cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">The default values for <code>refresh_key</code> are</span>
<ul>
<li><code>every: &#x27;2 minute&#x27;</code> for BigQuery, Athena, Snowflake, Presto, and Firebolt.</li>
<li><code>every: &#x27;10 second&#x27;</code> for all other databases.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Refresh key of a query is a concatenation of all cubes refresh keys involved in
query. For rollup queries pre-aggregation table name is used as a refresh key.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can set up a custom refresh check SQL by changing the <code>refresh_key</code> parameter.
Often, a <code>MAX(updated_at_timestamp)</code> for OLTP data is a viable option, or
examining a metadata table for whatever system is managing the data to see when
it last ran. timestamp in that case.</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" class="min-w-0 flex-1 w-0 rounded-tl-xl"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_1at5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content"><div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" role="tablist" aria-label="Code examples" class="text-xs leading-6 gap-1 flex"><button type="button" data-active="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" aria-disabled="false" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="true" id="base-ui-_R_5lat5deehkd74t9_" data-composite-item-active="" class="group flex items-center rounded-lg z-10 group-focus-visible:outline-1 group-focus-visible:outline-primary-light group-hover:bg-gray-700/70 group-hover:text-primary-light">YAML</div><div class="absolute -bottom-1.5 left-0 aria-disabled="false" tabindex="-1" role="tab" aria-selected="false" id="base-ui-_R_9lat5deehkd74t9_" class="group flex items-center relative gap-1.5 my-1 mb-1.5 outline-0 whitespace-nowrap font-medium ml-0! first:ml-2.5! focus-visible:outline-hidden text-gray-400"><div class="peer/title flex items-center gap-1.5 px-1.5 rounded-lg z-10 group-focus-visible:outline-1 <div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The <code>every</code> parameter guarantees that the refresh key will be executed <strong>at
least</strong> once during the specified interval. However, it does <strong>not</strong> guarantee
that it will be executed <strong>at most</strong> once. The refresh key may be checked more
frequently.</span><span data-as="p">When using <code>every</code> with <code>sql</code>, the purpose is to reduce the load from running
the refresh key query itself, not to minimize the number of cache
refreshes. The refresh key SQL should be designed to return consistent results
and only change when the underlying data needs to be updated, ensuring minimal
refreshes even if the query is executed multiple times.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><code>every</code> - can be set as an interval with granularities <code>second</code>, <code>minute</code>,
<code>hour</code>, <code>day</code>, and <code>week</code> or accept CRON string with some limitations. If you
set <code>every</code> as CRON string, you can use the <code>timezone</code> parameter. It takes
precedence over the query timezone.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 <span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  sql_table:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `orders`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  refresh_key:</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    every:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;30 5 * * 5&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    timezone:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;America/Los_Angeles&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><code>every</code> can accept only equal time intervals - so “Day of month” and “month”
intervals in CRON expressions are not supported.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Cube supports two different formats of CRON expressions: standard and advanced
with support for seconds.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Such <code>refresh_key</code> is just a syntactic sugar over <code>refresh_key</code> SQL. It’s
guaranteed that <code>refresh_key</code> change it’s value at least once during <code>every</code>
interval. It will be converted to appropriate SQL select which value will change
over time based on interval value. Values of interval based <code>refresh_key</code> are
tried to be checked ten times within defined interval but not more than once per
<code>1 second</code> and not less than once per <code>5 minute</code>. For example if interval is
<code>10 minute</code> it’s <code>refreshKeyRenewalThreshold</code> will be 60 seconds and generated
<code>refresh_key</code> SQL (Postgres) would be:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="1" language="sql"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center <ul>
<li>Standard cron syntax</li>

<span class="line"><span>└──────────────────── minute (0 - 59)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<ul>
<li>Advanced cron format with support for seconds</li>
</ul>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark <span class="line"><span>│    └──────────────────── minute (0 - 59)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>└───────────────────────── second (0 - 59, optional)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="meta"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#meta" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>meta</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Custom metadata. Can be used to pass any information to the frontend.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can also use the <code>ai_context</code> key to provide context to the
<a href="/docs/data-modeling/ai-context" class="link">AI agent</a> without exposing it in the user interface.</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  title:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `Product Orders`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="calendar"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#calendar" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>calendar</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>calendar</code> parameter is used to mark <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/calendar-cubes" class="link">calendar cubes</a>.
It’s set to <code>false</code> by default.</span>
<span data-as="p">When set to <code>true</code>, Cube will treat this cube as a calendar cube and allow to
<a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/calendar-cubes#overriding-time-shifts" class="link">override time-shifts</a> and <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/calendar-cubes#overriding-granularities" class="link">granularities</a>
on its <a href="/docs/data-modeling/dimensions#time-dimensions" class="link">time dimensions</a>. This can be useful for <a href="/docs/data-modeling/measures#time-shift" class="link">time-shift
calculations</a> with a custom calendar.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="pre_aggregations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#pre_aggregations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>pre_aggregations</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>pre_aggregations</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/pre-aggregations" class="link">pre-aggregations</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="joins"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#joins" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>joins</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>joins</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/joins" class="link">joins</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="dimensions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#dimensions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>dimensions</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>dimensions</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/dimensions" class="link">dimensions</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="hierarchies"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#hierarchies" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>hierarchies</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>hierarchies</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/hierarchies" class="link">hierarchies</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="segments"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#segments" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>segments</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>segments</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/segments" class="link">segments</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="measures"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#measures" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer"><code>measures</code></span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>measures</code> parameter is used to configure <a href="/reference/data-modeling/measures" class="link">measures</a>.</span>
